### Task 9: Web browsing pages (catalogue, product, stores, cart)

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/components/shop/product-card.tsx`
- Modify (replace the `ReservedPage` stub): `web/src/app/(shop)/products/page.tsx`, `web/src/app/(shop)/products/[id]/page.tsx`, `web/src/app/(shop)/stores/page.tsx`, `web/src/app/(shop)/cart/page.tsx`
- Modify: `web/src/lib/shop-pages.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 4: `GET /api/v1/products?q&category_id&brand_id&include_inactive&min_price&max_price&store_id` → `ProductSummary[]` with `store_quantity` (that store's quantity, 0 without an inventory row, `null` without `store_id`); 404 `not_found` for an unknown store; 422 `validation_error`, or 422 `invalid_price_range` when min is above max.
  - main: `GET /api/v1/products/{product_id}` → `ProductDetail` (`specs[{spec_name, spec_value}]`, `stock[{store_id, store_name, store_type, state, quantity}]` for every store); `GET /api/v1/stores`; `GET /api/v1/brands`; `useShop()` → `store`, `setStoreId`, `categories`; `CategorySelect({ categories, value, onChange, allLabel })`; `Badge, Button, Card, Empty, ErrorBox, Field, inputClass, selectClass, StockBadge, LOW_STOCK` (`@/components/ui`); `money`, `percentOff` (`@/lib/format`); `SHOP_PAGES`; `useLoad`.
  - Task 8: `useCart()` (`items, ready, count, addItem, setQuantity, removeItem`); `MAX_QUANTITY`, `fromCents`, `lineTotalCents` (`@/lib/cart`); type `ProductSummary` (`@/lib/api/client`, now with `store_quantity`).
- Produces:
  - `ProductCard({ product: ProductSummary; storeName?: string; onAdded?: (name: string) => void })` and `StoreLine({ quantity?: number | null; storeName?: string })` in `@/components/shop/product-card`.
  - Live pages: `/products` (filters in the URL: `q, category, brand, min_price, max_price`), `/products/[id]`, `/stores`, and `/cart`. The cart's Checkout link goes to `SHOP_PAGES.checkout.href`, which Task 10 builds.
  - `SHOP_PAGES.catalogue`, `.product`, `.cart` and `.stores` describe the built pages. The file's top comment no longer says "Most are reserved".

- [ ] **Step 1: `web/src/components/shop/product-card.tsx`**

Ported from `4a69838:web/src/components/shop/product-card.tsx`. "Add to cart" is no longer disabled when My store has none: stock is information, and a delivery can ship from another store (spec §6). Only a retired product can't be bought (BR07). Links come from `SHOP_PAGES`.

```tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";

import { Badge, Button, LOW_STOCK } from "@/components/ui";
import type { ProductSummary } from "@/lib/api/client";
import { useCart } from "@/lib/cart-context";
import { money, percentOff } from "@/lib/format";
import { SHOP_PAGES } from "@/lib/shop-pages";

/** One catalogue result: the current price, whether it is on sale, and the stock at "My store". */
export function ProductCard({ product, storeName, onAdded }: {
  product: ProductSummary;
  storeName?: string;
  onAdded?: (name: string) => void;
}) {
  const { addItem } = useCart();
  const href = `${SHOP_PAGES.catalogue.href}/${product.product_id}`;

  return (
    <article className="flex flex-col rounded-xl bg-white p-4 shadow-sm ring-1 ring-neutral-200">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
        {product.on_sale
          ? <Badge tone="sale">{percentOff(product.base_price, product.current_price)}</Badge>
          : <span />}
        {!product.is_active && <Badge>retired</Badge>}
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-neutral-500">{product.brand}</div>
      <Link href={href} className="line-clamp-2 font-medium text-neutral-900 hover:underline">{product.name}</Link>
      <div className="mt-auto pt-3">
        <span className="text-2xl font-bold">{money(product.current_price)}</span>
        {product.on_sale && (
          <s className="ml-2 text-sm text-neutral-500">
            <span className="sr-only">was </span>{money(product.base_price)}
          </s>
        )}
      </div>
      <StoreLine quantity={product.store_quantity} storeName={storeName} />
      <div className="mt-3 flex gap-2">
        {/* Stock at My store is information, not a limit: a delivery can ship from another store, and
            checkout takes the stock in the database. Only a retired product can't be bought (BR07). */}
        <Button tone="primary" disabled={!product.is_active}
          onClick={() => {
            addItem(product.product_id);
            onAdded?.(product.name);
          }}>
          Add to cart
        </Button>
        <Link href={href}
          className="inline-flex h-9 items-center rounded-md px-3 text-sm font-medium text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-100">
          Details
        </Link>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

/** Stock at one store. Unknown (no store chosen yet) is not the same as none. */
export function StoreLine({ quantity, storeName }: { quantity?: number | null; storeName?: string }) {
  if (quantity === undefined || quantity === null || !storeName) {
    return <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-neutral-400">Choose a store to see its stock</div>;
  }
  const [dot, text] = quantity === 0 ? ["bg-red-500", `Out of stock at ${storeName}`]
    : quantity <= LOW_STOCK ? ["bg-amber-500", `Only ${quantity} left at ${storeName}`]
    : ["bg-emerald-500", `${quantity} in stock at ${storeName}`];
  return (
    <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-neutral-600">
      <span className={`h-2 w-2 rounded-full ${dot}`} /> {text}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `web/src/app/(shop)/products/page.tsx`**

This replaces the stub. It is adapted from `4a69838:web/src/app/(shop)/products/page.tsx`.

Kept from the branch:
- The URL is the only state for the filters (`useSearchParams` and `router.replace(…, { scroll: false })`).
- The page sits inside a `<Suspense>` boundary. Next 16 refuses to prerender a page that calls `useSearchParams` outside one (`web/node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/03-api-reference/04-functions/use-search-params.md`).
- The card grid, the "Added … Go to cart" line, and the empty and error states.

Changed:
- **Category:** main's two-level `CategorySelect` over `useShop().categories`. The branch's recursive `categoryOptions` and its second `GET /categories` are not ported, because the API already includes a top-level category's subcategories.
- **Search and price boxes:** they sit in a `<form>` that applies on Enter or "Apply", keyed on the applied values. Next 16 keeps a page mounted when only its query changes (`next/dist/client/components/layout-router.js`: "search params do not cause state to be lost"). The branch's uncontrolled boxes, applied on every keystroke, therefore kept stale text after a header search, "Clear filters" or back/forward.
- **Price boxes:** they are `type="number" min=0 step=0.01`, so the browser refuses a negative price or a third decimal. A minimum above the maximum is left to the API's 422 `invalid_price_range`, shown in the error box.
- **Stock:** `store_id` is My store, so each card's stock line comes from `store_quantity`.

```tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { type FormEvent, Suspense, useState } from "react";

import { CategorySelect } from "@/components/category-select";
import { useShop } from "@/components/shop/context";
import { ProductCard } from "@/components/shop/product-card";
import { Button, Card, Empty, ErrorBox, Field, inputClass, selectClass } from "@/components/ui";
import { api, call } from "@/lib/api/client";
import { SHOP_PAGES } from "@/lib/shop-pages";
import { useLoad } from "@/lib/use-load";

// The filters live in the URL, so the header's search box (?q=), the home page's category links (?category=),
// back/forward and a shared link all show the same results.
const FILTERS = ["q", "category", "brand", "min_price", "max_price"] as const;
type Filters = Record<(typeof FILTERS)[number], string>;

export default function CataloguePage() {
  // useSearchParams needs a Suspense boundary, or `next build` can't prerender the page.
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<p className="text-sm text-neutral-500">Loading the catalogue…</p>}>
      <Catalogue />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

function Catalogue() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const params = useSearchParams();
  const { store, categories } = useShop();
  const [added, setAdded] = useState<string>();
  const filters = Object.fromEntries(FILTERS.map((name) => [name, params.get(name) ?? ""])) as Filters;
  const filtered = FILTERS.some((name) => filters[name]);

  /** Show the catalogue with these filters changed; an empty value removes a filter. */
  const show = (changes: Partial<Filters>) => {
    const next = new URLSearchParams(params.toString());
    for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(changes)) {
      if (value) next.set(name, value);
      else next.delete(name);
    }
    const query = next.toString();
    router.replace(query ? `${SHOP_PAGES.catalogue.href}?${query}` : SHOP_PAGES.catalogue.href, { scroll: false });
  };
  const clear = () => router.replace(SHOP_PAGES.catalogue.href, { scroll: false });

  // The search and price boxes apply together, on Enter or "Apply"; the two lists apply as soon as they change.
  const apply = (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const form = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    const text = (name: string) => String(form.get(name) ?? "").trim();
    show({ q: text("q"), min_price: text("min_price"), max_price: text("max_price") });
  };

  const brands = useLoad("brands", () => call(api.GET("/api/v1/brands")));
  const categoryId = filters.category ? Number(filters.category) : undefined;
  const storeId = store?.store_id;
  // Prices go as typed: the API answers 422 for a malformed one, and 422 invalid_price_range for min above max.
  const products = useLoad(JSON.stringify(["products", filters, storeId]), () =>
    call(api.GET("/api/v1/products", {
      params: {
        query: {
          q: filters.q || undefined,
          category_id: categoryId,
          brand_id: filters.brand ? Number(filters.brand) : undefined,
          min_price: filters.min_price || undefined,
          max_price: filters.max_price || undefined,
          store_id: storeId,
        },
      },
    })));
  const rows = products.data ?? [];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <title>Shop all products · JB Hi-Fi</title>
      <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="text-sm text-neutral-500">
        <Link href="/" className="hover:underline">Home</Link> / Shop all products
      </nav>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Shop all products</h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-neutral-600">
            Prices are the current price — the sale price while a promotion runs. Stock is for{" "}
            <b>{store?.name ?? "your store"}</b>; change it under “My store” above.
          </p>
        </div>
        {products.data && (
          <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600">
            {rows.length} {rows.length === 1 ? "product" : "products"}{filtered && " match"}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      <Card>
        {/* Keyed on the applied values: the page stays mounted when only the URL's query changes, so after a
            header search, "Clear filters" or back/forward the boxes are re-created showing what the URL says. */}
        <form key={JSON.stringify([filters.q, filters.min_price, filters.max_price])} onSubmit={apply}
          className="grid items-end gap-3 p-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-[2fr_repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr))_auto]">
          <Field label="Search">
            <input name="q" type="search" className={inputClass} defaultValue={filters.q}
              placeholder="Name, SKU or brand" />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Category">
            <CategorySelect categories={categories} allLabel="All categories" value={categoryId}
              onChange={(id) => show({ category: id === undefined ? "" : String(id) })} />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Brand">
            <select className={selectClass} value={filters.brand}
              onChange={(event) => show({ brand: event.target.value })}>
              <option value="">All brands</option>
              {(brands.data ?? []).map((brand) => (
                <option key={brand.brand_id} value={brand.brand_id}>{brand.name}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </Field>
          <Field label="Price from ($)">
            <input name="min_price" type="number" min={0} step="0.01" className={inputClass}
              defaultValue={filters.min_price} placeholder="0" />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Price to ($)">
            <input name="max_price" type="number" min={0} step="0.01" className={inputClass}
              defaultValue={filters.max_price} placeholder="Any" />
          </Field>
          <Button type="submit" tone="primary">Apply</Button>
        </form>
        {(filtered || brands.error) && (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 border-t border-neutral-200 px-4 py-3">
            {filtered && <Button size="sm" onClick={clear}>Clear filters</Button>}
            {brands.error && <ErrorBox error={brands.error} />}
          </div>
        )}
      </Card>

      {added && (
        <p role="status" className="rounded-lg border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-emerald-900">
          Added <b>{added}</b> to your cart.{" "}
          <Link href={SHOP_PAGES.cart.href} className="font-medium underline">Go to cart →</Link>
        </p>
      )}

      {products.error && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <ErrorBox error={products.error} />
          <Button onClick={products.reload}>Try again</Button>
        </div>
      )}

      {!products.error && !products.data && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4">
          {Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, index) => (
            <div key={index} className="h-64 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-neutral-200" />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {!products.error && products.data && rows.length === 0 && (
        <Card><Empty>
          Nothing matches those filters.{" "}
          <button type="button" className="font-medium underline" onClick={clear}>Clear them</button>.
        </Empty></Card>
      )}

      {!products.error && (
        <div className={`grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4
          ${products.loading ? "opacity-60" : ""}`}>
          {rows.map((product) => (
            <ProductCard key={product.product_id} product={product} storeName={store?.name} onAdded={setAdded} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `web/src/app/(shop)/products/[id]/page.tsx`**

This replaces the stub. It is adapted from `4a69838:web/src/app/(shop)/products/[id]/page.tsx`. Changed:
- The product is shown only when the loaded id matches this page's id: main's `useLoad` keeps the previous result while the next one loads.
- "Add to cart" stays enabled when My store has none.
- The quantity is capped at `MAX_QUANTITY`, so "Added N" matches what the cart holds.
- The branch's second store picker ("Collect from / check stock at") is dropped, because it duplicated the header's "My store".

```tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";

import { useShop } from "@/components/shop/context";
import { StoreLine } from "@/components/shop/product-card";
import { Badge, Button, Card, Empty, ErrorBox, inputClass, StockBadge } from "@/components/ui";
import { api, call } from "@/lib/api/client";
import { MAX_QUANTITY } from "@/lib/cart";
import { useCart } from "@/lib/cart-context";
import { money, percentOff } from "@/lib/format";
import { SHOP_PAGES } from "@/lib/shop-pages";
import { useLoad } from "@/lib/use-load";

export default function ProductPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const productId = Number(id);
  const valid = Number.isInteger(productId) && productId > 0;
  const { store } = useShop();
  const { addItem } = useCart();
  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState("1");
  const [added, setAdded] = useState<number>();

  const product = useLoad(`product:${id}`, () =>
    valid
      ? call(api.GET("/api/v1/products/{product_id}", { params: { path: { product_id: productId } } }))
      : Promise.resolve(undefined));
  // useLoad keeps the previous result while a new one loads: never show another product under this id.
  const detail = product.data?.product_id === productId ? product.data : undefined;

  if (!valid) {
    return (
      <Card><Empty>
        That isn&apos;t a product number.{" "}
        <Link href={SHOP_PAGES.catalogue.href} className="underline">Back to the shop</Link>.
      </Empty></Card>
    );
  }

  const units = Math.min(Math.max(Math.trunc(Number(quantity)) || 1, 1), MAX_QUANTITY);
  // My store first (it is the one the stock line quotes), then the rest in store order.
  const stock = [...(detail?.stock ?? [])].sort((a, b) =>
    Number(b.store_id === store?.store_id) - Number(a.store_id === store?.store_id));
  const mine = stock.find((row) => row.store_id === store?.store_id);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <title>{`${detail?.name ?? "Product"} · JB Hi-Fi`}</title>
      <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="text-sm text-neutral-500">
        <Link href="/" className="hover:underline">Home</Link> /{" "}
        <Link href={SHOP_PAGES.catalogue.href} className="hover:underline">Shop all products</Link> /{" "}
        {detail?.name ?? "…"}
      </nav>

      {product.error && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <ErrorBox error={product.error} />
          <Button onClick={product.reload}>Try again</Button>
        </div>
      )}
      {!product.error && !detail && <div className="h-40 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-neutral-200" />}

      {detail && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,2fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
          <div className="space-y-5">
            <Card className="p-6">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
                <div>
                  <div className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-neutral-500">{detail.brand}</div>
                  <h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">{detail.name}</h1>
                  <div className="mt-1 font-mono text-xs text-neutral-500">{detail.sku}</div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  {detail.on_sale && <Badge tone="sale">{percentOff(detail.base_price, detail.current_price)}</Badge>}
                  {!detail.is_active && <Badge>retired</Badge>}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
                <span className="text-3xl font-bold">{money(detail.current_price)}</span>
                {detail.on_sale && (
                  <s className="text-neutral-500"><span className="sr-only">was </span>{money(detail.base_price)}</s>
                )}
              </div>

              {!detail.is_active && (
                <p className="mt-4 rounded-lg border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-amber-900">
                  This product has been retired, so it can&apos;t be bought (BR07). Its record is kept because past
                  orders point to it.
                </p>
              )}

              <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
                <label className="block w-28">
                  <span className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-neutral-500">
                    Quantity
                  </span>
                  <input className={inputClass} type="number" min={1} max={MAX_QUANTITY} step={1} value={quantity}
                    onChange={(event) => setQuantity(event.target.value)} />
                </label>
                {/* Enabled even when My store has none: a delivery can ship from another store. */}
                <Button tone="primary" disabled={!detail.is_active}
                  onClick={() => {
                    addItem(detail.product_id, units);
                    setAdded(units);
                  }}>
                  Add to cart
                </Button>
                <Link href={SHOP_PAGES.cart.href}
                  className="inline-flex h-9 items-center rounded-md px-3 text-sm font-medium text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-100">
                  Go to cart
                </Link>
              </div>
              {added !== undefined && (
                <p role="status" className="mt-3 text-sm text-emerald-800">
                  Added {added} to your cart. Stock is only taken when checkout commits.
                </p>
              )}
              {mine && (
                <div className="mt-4 border-t border-neutral-200 pt-3">
                  <StoreLine quantity={mine.quantity} storeName={store?.name} />
                </div>
              )}
            </Card>

            <Card title="Specifications">
              {detail.specs.length === 0
                ? <Empty>No specifications recorded for this product.</Empty>
                : (
                  <dl className="divide-y divide-neutral-100">
                    {detail.specs.map((spec) => (
                      <div key={spec.spec_name} className="flex gap-4 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm">
                        <dt className="w-40 shrink-0 text-neutral-500">{spec.spec_name}</dt>
                        <dd className="text-neutral-900">{spec.spec_value}</dd>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </dl>
                )}
            </Card>
          </div>

          <Card title="Where it is in stock" actions={<span className="text-xs text-neutral-500">R3</span>}>
            <ul className="divide-y divide-neutral-100">
              {stock.map((row) => {
                const isMine = row.store_id === store?.store_id;
                return (
                  <li key={row.store_id}
                    className={`flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm ${isMine ? "bg-yellow-50" : ""}`}>
                    <div>
                      <div className="font-medium text-neutral-900">
                        {row.store_name}
                        {row.store_type === "warehouse" && (
                          <span className="ml-2"><Badge tone="blue">warehouse</Badge></span>
                        )}
                        {isMine && <span className="ml-2"><Badge tone="sale">your store</Badge></span>}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-neutral-500">{row.state}</div>
                    </div>
                    <StockBadge quantity={row.quantity} />
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
            <p className="border-t border-neutral-200 px-4 py-3 text-xs text-neutral-500">
              To collect from another store, change “My store” at the top of the page.
            </p>
          </Card>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `web/src/app/(shop)/stores/page.tsx`**

This replaces the stub. It is adapted from `4a69838:web/src/app/(shop)/stores/page.tsx`. Changed:
- "Who has it?" searches by `q` alone. The stock comes from the product detail, so the branch's `store_id` only caused extra reloads.
- The stock box shows only the chosen product (the same `useLoad` guard as the product page).
- The error box shows this page's own store-list error only.

```tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";

import { useShop } from "@/components/shop/context";
import { Badge, Button, Card, Empty, ErrorBox, inputClass, StockBadge } from "@/components/ui";
import { api, call } from "@/lib/api/client";
import { useLoad } from "@/lib/use-load";

export default function StoresPage() {
  const { store, setStoreId } = useShop();
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [chosen, setChosen] = useState<number>();
  const term = search.trim();

  // All stores, the warehouse too (the shop context keeps only the retail ones).
  const stores = useLoad("all-stores", () => call(api.GET("/api/v1/stores")));
  const matches = useLoad(`store-lookup:${term}`, () =>
    term.length < 2 ? Promise.resolve([]) : call(api.GET("/api/v1/products", { params: { query: { q: term } } })));
  const detail = useLoad(`store-lookup-product:${chosen}`, () =>
    chosen === undefined
      ? Promise.resolve(undefined)
      : call(api.GET("/api/v1/products/{product_id}", { params: { path: { product_id: chosen } } })));
  // useLoad keeps the previous product while the next loads: show only the one chosen.
  const product = detail.data?.product_id === chosen ? detail.data : undefined;

  const states = [...new Set((stores.data ?? []).map((row) => row.state))].sort();

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <title>Stores · JB Hi-Fi</title>
      <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="text-sm text-neutral-500">
        <Link href="/" className="hover:underline">Home</Link> / Stores
      </nav>

      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Stores</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-neutral-600">
          Click &amp; Collect is offered by the retail stores only — never the warehouse (BR06). Your store is where
          stock is quoted and where you would pick an order up.
        </p>
      </div>

      {stores.error && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <ErrorBox error={stores.error} />
          <Button onClick={stores.reload}>Try again</Button>
        </div>
      )}

      <Card title="Who has it?" actions={<span className="text-xs text-neutral-500">R3</span>}>
        <div className="space-y-3 p-4">
          <label className="block max-w-md">
            <span className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-neutral-500">
              Search the catalogue by name, brand or SKU
            </span>
            <input className={inputClass} value={search} placeholder="e.g. Sony or SONY-WH1000XM5-B"
              onChange={(event) => {
                setSearch(event.target.value);
                setChosen(undefined);
              }} />
          </label>

          {term.length >= 2 && (matches.loading && !matches.data
            ? <p className="text-sm text-neutral-500">Searching…</p>
            : (matches.data ?? []).length === 0
              ? <p className="text-sm text-neutral-500">Nothing matches “{term}”.</p>
              : (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  {(matches.data ?? []).slice(0, 8).map((match) => (
                    <Button key={match.product_id} size="sm"
                      tone={chosen === match.product_id ? "primary" : "secondary"}
                      onClick={() => setChosen(match.product_id)}>
                      {match.brand} {match.name}
                    </Button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              ))}
          {matches.error && <ErrorBox error={matches.error} />}

          {chosen !== undefined && (
            <div className="rounded-lg border border-neutral-200">
              {detail.error && <ErrorBox error={detail.error} className="m-3" />}
              {!detail.error && !product && <p className="p-4 text-sm text-neutral-500">Loading stock…</p>}
              {product && (
                <>
                  <div className="border-b border-neutral-200 px-4 py-3">
                    <div className="font-medium">{product.brand} {product.name}</div>
                    <div className="font-mono text-xs text-neutral-500">{product.sku}</div>
                  </div>
                  <ul className="divide-y divide-neutral-100">
                    {product.stock.map((row) => (
                      <li key={row.store_id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-2 text-sm">
                        <span>
                          {row.store_name}
                          {row.store_type === "warehouse" && (
                            <span className="ml-2"><Badge tone="blue">warehouse</Badge></span>
                          )}
                        </span>
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-3">
                          <span className="text-xs text-neutral-500">{row.state}</span>
                          <StockBadge quantity={row.quantity} />
                        </span>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </Card>

      {!stores.data && !stores.error && <div className="h-40 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-neutral-200" />}
      {stores.data?.length === 0 && <Card><Empty>No stores to show.</Empty></Card>}

      {states.map((state) => (
        <section key={state}>
          <h2 className="mb-3 text-lg font-semibold tracking-tight">{state}</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
            {(stores.data ?? []).filter((row) => row.state === state).map((row) => {
              const isMine = row.store_id === store?.store_id;
              const retail = row.store_type === "retail";
              return (
                <div key={row.store_id}
                  className={`rounded-xl bg-white p-4 shadow-sm ring-1 ${isMine ? "ring-2 ring-neutral-900" : "ring-neutral-200"}`}>
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                    <div>
                      <div className="font-semibold">{row.name}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-neutral-500">{row.suburb} {row.state}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1">
                      {isMine && <Badge tone="sale">your store</Badge>}
                      {!retail && <Badge tone="blue">warehouse</Badge>}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-neutral-500">Manager: {row.manager_name}</div>
                  {retail && !isMine && (
                    <button type="button" onClick={() => setStoreId(row.store_id)}
                      className="mt-3 text-sm font-medium text-neutral-800 hover:underline">
                      Make this my store
                    </button>
                  )}
                  {!retail && (
                    <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-neutral-500">Ships online orders; not a collection point.</p>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </section>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `web/src/app/(shop)/cart/page.tsx`**

This replaces the stub. It is adapted from `4a69838:web/src/app/(shop)/cart/page.tsx`.

Kept from the branch:
- One `GET /products?include_inactive=true&store_id=<My store>` supplies the prices and stock.
- Totals are in integer cents.
- Retired and unknown products are flagged and block the Checkout link.

Changed:
- A line counts as short only when `store_quantity` is a number. Before My store is known it is `null`, which the branch counted as 0, so it warned on every line.
- A −/+ stepper replaces the number box. In the branch an emptied box read as `Number("") = 0`, and `setQuantity(…, 0)` removed the line.
- The shortfall note now says checkout re-checks stock at the store that fulfils the order, because T1 takes stock only there. The branch said "all stores".

```tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";

import { useShop } from "@/components/shop/context";
import { Badge, Button, Card, Empty, ErrorBox } from "@/components/ui";
import { api, call, type ProductSummary } from "@/lib/api/client";
import { fromCents, lineTotalCents, MAX_QUANTITY } from "@/lib/cart";
import { useCart } from "@/lib/cart-context";
import { money } from "@/lib/format";
import { SHOP_PAGES } from "@/lib/shop-pages";
import { useLoad } from "@/lib/use-load";

export default function CartPage() {
  const { store } = useShop();
  const { items, ready, count, setQuantity, removeItem } = useCart();
  const storeId = store?.store_id;

  // One request for the whole catalogue — retired products too, so they can be flagged — with My store's stock.
  const products = useLoad(`cart-products:${storeId}`, () =>
    call(api.GET("/api/v1/products", { params: { query: { include_inactive: true, store_id: storeId } } })));

  const byId = new Map<number, ProductSummary>((products.data ?? []).map((row) => [row.product_id, row]));
  const lines = items.map((item) => ({ ...item, product: byId.get(item.product_id) }));
  const unknown = products.data ? lines.filter((line) => !line.product) : [];
  const retired = lines.filter((line) => line.product && !line.product.is_active);
  // Integer cents, so the subtotal is exact (lib/cart.ts).
  const subtotal = lines.reduce((total, line) =>
    total + (line.product ? lineTotalCents(line.product.current_price, line.quantity) : 0), 0);
  // Stock is only known once My store is (store_quantity is null without a store_id).
  const short = lines.filter((line) =>
    typeof line.product?.store_quantity === "number" && line.product.store_quantity < line.quantity);
  const blocked = !ready || items.length === 0 || !products.data || unknown.length > 0 || retired.length > 0;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <title>Cart · JB Hi-Fi</title>
      <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="text-sm text-neutral-500">
        <Link href="/" className="hover:underline">Home</Link> /{" "}
        <Link href={SHOP_PAGES.catalogue.href} className="hover:underline">Shop all products</Link> / Cart
      </nav>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Your cart</h1>

      {products.error && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <ErrorBox error={products.error} />
          <Button onClick={products.reload}>Try again</Button>
        </div>
      )}

      {ready && items.length === 0 && (
        <Card><Empty>
          Your cart is empty.{" "}
          <Link href={SHOP_PAGES.catalogue.href} className="font-medium underline">Find something to buy →</Link>
        </Empty></Card>
      )}

      {items.length > 0 && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,2fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
          <Card title={`${count} ${count === 1 ? "item" : "items"}`}
            actions={(
              <span className="text-xs text-neutral-500">Current prices; stock at {store?.name ?? "your store"}</span>
            )}>
            <ul className="divide-y divide-neutral-100">
              {lines.map((line) => {
                const price = line.product?.current_price;
                const stock = line.product?.store_quantity;
                return (
                  <li key={line.product_id} className="flex flex-wrap items-start gap-4 px-4 py-4">
                    <div className="min-w-[12rem] flex-1">
                      {line.product ? (
                        <>
                          <div className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-neutral-500">
                            {line.product.brand}
                          </div>
                          <Link href={`${SHOP_PAGES.catalogue.href}/${line.product_id}`}
                            className="font-medium hover:underline">
                            {line.product.name}
                          </Link>
                          <div className="font-mono text-xs text-neutral-500">{line.product.sku}</div>
                          {!line.product.is_active && (
                            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-amber-700">
                              Retired, so it can&apos;t be bought — remove it to continue.
                            </p>
                          )}
                          {line.product.is_active && typeof stock === "number" && stock < line.quantity && (
                            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-amber-700">
                              Only {stock} at {store?.name ?? "your store"} right now. Checkout will tell you for
                              certain.
                            </p>
                          )}
                        </>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          <div className="font-medium text-neutral-900">Product #{line.product_id}</div>
                          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-red-700">
                            {products.data ? "No longer in the catalogue — remove it to continue." : "Loading…"}
                          </p>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    {/* Steps of one, never below 1: only "Remove" takes a line out (an emptied number box would
                        have read as 0 and removed it). */}
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                      <Button size="sm" aria-label="One fewer" disabled={line.quantity <= 1}
                        onClick={() => setQuantity(line.product_id, line.quantity - 1)}>−</Button>
                      <span className="w-8 text-center text-sm tabular-nums">{line.quantity}</span>
                      <Button size="sm" aria-label="One more" disabled={line.quantity >= MAX_QUANTITY}
                        onClick={() => setQuantity(line.product_id, line.quantity + 1)}>+</Button>
                    </div>

                    <div className="w-24 text-right">
                      {price && (
                        <>
                          <div className="font-semibold tabular-nums">
                            {money(fromCents(lineTotalCents(price, line.quantity)))}
                          </div>
                          <div className="text-xs text-neutral-500">{money(price)} each</div>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <button type="button" onClick={() => removeItem(line.product_id)}
                      className="text-sm text-neutral-500 underline hover:text-neutral-800">
                      Remove
                    </button>
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
          </Card>

          <Card title="Summary">
            <div className="space-y-3 p-4">
              <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
                <span className="text-sm text-neutral-600">Subtotal ({count} {count === 1 ? "item" : "items"})</span>
                <span className="text-xl font-bold tabular-nums">{money(fromCents(subtotal))}</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500">
                Prices include any running promotion. Nothing is reserved until checkout commits, and the amount
                charged is worked out by the database, not this page.
              </p>
              {short.length > 0 && (
                <p className="rounded-lg border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-amber-900">
                  {short.length === 1 ? "One item is" : `${short.length} items are`} short at{" "}
                  {store?.name ?? "your store"}. You can still try: checkout re-checks the stock at the store that
                  fulfils the order, in the same transaction.
                </p>
              )}
              <Link href={SHOP_PAGES.checkout.href} aria-disabled={blocked}
                onClick={(event) => {
                  if (blocked) event.preventDefault();
                }}
                className={`inline-flex h-9 w-full items-center justify-center rounded-md text-sm font-medium text-white
                  ${blocked ? "cursor-not-allowed bg-neutral-400" : "bg-neutral-900 hover:bg-neutral-700"}`}>
                Checkout
              </Link>
              <Link href={SHOP_PAGES.catalogue.href}
                className="block text-center text-sm font-medium text-neutral-700 hover:underline">
                Continue shopping
              </Link>
              {unknown.length + retired.length > 0 && (
                <p className="text-xs text-red-700">Remove the items marked above before checking out.</p>
              )}
              {!products.data && !products.error && <Badge>Checking prices…</Badge>}
            </div>
          </Card>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

The dev database keeps main's schema until Task 11, but these pages read only tables and views that already exist there. Browse as a guest at http://localhost:3001. It is a new origin, so "My store" starts at Sydney CBD. The numbers below are for the seeded data.

1. `/products` lists 48 products, each with a stock line "… at Sydney CBD".

Only "Flip 7 Portable Bluetooth Speaker (Black)" is listed, at $149.95 with $199.95 struck through: the filter is on the current price, and its base price is outside the range. Click its "Add to cart": the page says "Added … to your cart. Go to cart →" and the header shows "Cart 1".
4. Set the price from 600 to 100 and press Apply. The error box says "Request failed" with the code `invalid_price_range`.
5. Clear the filters and pick the category "Audio": 10 products match, including its Headphones and Speakers subcategories. Picking a brand narrows the list further.
6. Change "My store" in the header to Chadstone. The stock lines now name Chadstone. Change it back to Sydney CBD.
7. `/products/44` (Sony WH-1000XM5) shows:
   - $399.00 with $549.95 struck through, and "27% off";
   - the specifications;
   - 13 stores under "Where it is in stock", with My store first and highlighted and Eastern Creek DC tagged "warehouse".

   Set the quantity to 3 and press Add to cart: the page says "Added 3 to your cart" and the header shows "Cart 4".
8. `/products/8` (the iPad 11-inch) shows "Out of stock at Sydney CBD", and Add to cart is still enabled.
9. `/products/abc` says "That isn't a product number". `/products/99999` shows an error box with `not_found`.
10. `/stores`:
    - It has NSW, QLD and VIC sections.
    - Eastern Creek DC is tagged "warehouse", with "Ships online orders; not a collection point".
    - "Make this my store" on Bondi Junction changes the header picker.
    - In "Who has it?", type "flip" and pick the JBL button: its stock at all 13 stores appears.
11. `/cart`:
    - It shows Flip 7 × 1 at $149.95 and WH-1000XM5 × 3 at $1,197.00, with a subtotal of $1,346.95.
    - − is disabled at 1, + adds one, and Remove takes a line out.
    - Checkout opens `/checkout`, which is still reserved until Task 10.
12. In DevTools, run `localStorage.setItem("jb.shop.cart", JSON.stringify({ version: 1, items: [{ product_id: 999, quantity: 1 }] }))` and reload `/cart`. It shows "Product #999" and "No longer in the catalogue — remove it to continue", and the Checkout link is disabled. Remove the line and the page says "Your cart is empty."

main's sign-in (`useMe` / `ShopProvider`) and API client, plus the home page's "My orders" card. Spec §6.

- [ ] **Step 2: `web/src/components/shop/context.tsx` — every store, and the customer's own load**

Three things the pages need that main's provider doesn't give:
- the warehouse, so delivery can ship from it: the provider already loads every store but exposes only the retail ones;
- the customer's own load failing: on main it silently ends as "no customer", so the pages would say "sign in" to a
  signed-in customer;
- re-reading the customer after an address is added or retired: main's `reload()` refreshes the session, stores and
  categories, never the customer.

Task 8 changed only the top of this file (the storage helpers moved to `@/lib/browser-storage`). Replace everything from
`type Shop = {` to the end of the file with:

```tsx
type Shop = {
  /** Who is signed in, as the API sees them (a Clerk sign-in, mapped to our data by GET /me). */
  session: MeState;
  /** The signed-in customer, with addresses; undefined for a guest, for staff, or while loading. */
  customer?: CustomerDetail;
  /** Still finding out who is signed in, or their details haven't arrived yet. */
  customerLoading: boolean;
  /** The signed-in customer's own record failed to load: pages show the error instead of "sign in". */
  customerError?: ApiError;
  /** Read the signed-in customer's record again, e.g. after adding or retiring an address. */
  reloadCustomer: () => void;
  /** Every store, the warehouse included: a delivery can ship from any store with stock. */
  stores: Store[];
  /** Stores a customer can shop at: retail only — the warehouse doesn't do Click & Collect (BR06). */
  retailStores: Store[];
  /** "My store": the one chosen, else the first in the customer's state, else the first store.
   *  undefined while stores (or the signed-in customer, whose state decides it) are still loading. */
  store?: Store;
  /** undefined goes back to the default (nearest store). */
  setStoreId: (id: number | undefined) => void;
  /** State of the customer's current address, used for "stores near you". */
  homeState?: string;
  categories: Category[];
  /** Who-am-I, stores or categories failed to load (e.g. the API isn't running). */
  loadError?: ApiError;
  /** Try those loads again, and the customer's record. */
  reload: () => void;
};

const ShopContext = createContext<Shop | null>(null);

export function useShop(): Shop {
  const shop = useContext(ShopContext);
  if (!shop) throw new Error("useShop() must be used inside <ShopProvider>");
  return shop;
}

export function ShopProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const session = useMe();
  const [storeId, setStoreId] = useStoredId("jb.shop.storeId");
  const customerId = session.status === "signed-in" && session.me.kind === "customer" ? session.me.id : undefined;

  const stores = useLoad("stores", () => call(api.GET("/api/v1/stores")));
  const categories = useLoad("categories", () => call(api.GET("/api/v1/categories")));
  const detail = useLoad(`customer:${customerId}`, () =>
    customerId === undefined
      ? Promise.resolve(undefined)
      : call(api.GET("/api/v1/customers/{customer_id}", { params: { path: { customer_id: customerId } } })));

  // While another customer loads, don't show the previous one's details under the new name.
  const customer = detail.data?.customer_id === customerId ? detail.data : undefined;
  const homeState = customer?.addresses.find((a) => a.is_active)?.state;
  const allStores = stores.data ?? [];
  const retailStores = allStores.filter((s) => s.store_type === "retail");
  const customerLoading = session.status === "loading" || (customerId !== undefined && !customer && !detail.error);
  // Don't guess a store from the first one while the customer's state is still on its way.
  const store = retailStores.find((s) => s.store_id === storeId)
    ?? (customerLoading ? undefined : retailStores.find((s) => s.state === homeState) ?? retailStores[0]);

  const shop: Shop = {
    session,
    customer,
    customerLoading,
    customerError: detail.error,
    reloadCustomer: detail.reload,
    stores: allStores,
    retailStores,
    store,
    setStoreId,
    homeState,
    categories: categories.data ?? [],
    loadError: (session.status === "error" ? session.error : undefined) ?? stores.error ?? categories.error,
    reload: () => {
      if (session.status === "error") session.retry();
      stores.reload();
      categories.reload();
      detail.reload();
    },
  };
  return <ShopContext value={shop}>{children}</ShopContext>;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `web/src/components/shop/customer-gate.tsx`**

The branch repeated "Checking who is signed in… / Sign in (demo) on the home page" on every page. One gate replaces
that, like main's `StaffGate`. A guest gets Clerk's sign-in in a modal, so they stay on the page and the cart — kept in
this browser, not in the account — is still there afterwards. Staff get a note on the customer pages (they have no
customer record for the page to ask the API about); the order page uses `SignedInGate`, so staff and support see
whatever `GET /orders/{id}` answers them (spec §6).

```tsx
"use client";

import { SignInButton } from "@clerk/nextjs";
import Link from "next/link";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import { useShop } from "@/components/shop/context";
import { Button, Card, ErrorBox } from "@/components/ui";
import type { ApiError, CustomerDetail, Me } from "@/lib/api/client";

// Gates only decide what a page shows. The API checks every request itself (app/auth.py), whatever is on screen.

/** Anyone signed in. The page then asks the API, which decides what this person may see (an order). */
export function SignedInGate({ what, children }: { what: string; children: (me: Me) => ReactNode }) {
  const { session } = useShop();
  if (session.status === "loading") return <Checking />;
  if (session.status === "error") return <Failed error={session.error} retry={session.retry} />;
  if (session.status === "guest") return <SignInPrompt what={what} />;
  return children(session.me);
}

/** A signed-in customer, with their record (and addresses): checkout, My orders, My account. */
export function CustomerGate({ what, children }: {
  what: string;
  children: (customer: CustomerDetail) => ReactNode;
}) {
  const { session, customer, customerLoading, customerError, reloadCustomer } = useShop();
  if (session.status === "error") return <Failed error={session.error} retry={session.retry} />;
  if (session.status === "guest") return <SignInPrompt what={what} />;
  if (session.status === "signed-in" && session.me.kind === "staff") {
    return (
      <Card>
        <p className="px-4 py-10 text-center text-sm text-neutral-600">
          You&apos;re signed in as staff, and this page is for customer accounts.{" "}
          <Link href="/admin" className="font-medium underline">Go to the store admin →</Link>
        </p>
      </Card>
    );
  }
  if (customerError) return <Failed error={customerError} retry={reloadCustomer} />;
  if (customerLoading || !customer) return <Checking />;
  return children(customer);
}

function Checking() {
  return <p className="text-sm text-neutral-500">Checking who is signed in…</p>;
}

function Failed({ error, retry }: { error: ApiError; retry: () => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
      <ErrorBox error={error} />
      <Button onClick={retry}>Try again</Button>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Clerk's sign-in opens in a modal, so the visitor stays on this page, and the cart (kept in this browser, not
 *  in the account) is still there afterwards. */
function SignInPrompt({ what }: { what: string }) {
  return (
    <Card>
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 px-4 py-10 text-center text-sm text-neutral-600">
        <p>Sign in to {what}. Your cart stays as it is.</p>
        <SignInButton mode="modal">
          <button type="button"
            className="h-9 rounded-md bg-neutral-900 px-4 font-semibold text-white hover:bg-neutral-700">
            Sign in
          </button>
        </SignInButton>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `web/src/components/shop/order-timeline.tsx`**

Adapted from the branch: the return statuses and the `ReturnStatus` import are gone (SP3), `StatusBadge` takes any
status string, the timeline sorts by `seq` (checkout's `placed` and `paid` share one `now()`), and `NextStatuses`
shows `allowed_next_statuses` as text.

```tsx
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui";
import type { OrderDetail } from "@/lib/api/client";
import { formatDateTime } from "@/lib/format";

type HistoryEntry = OrderDetail["history"][number];

/** Plain-language names for the database's order statuses, so staff and customers see the same words. */
export const ORDER_STATUS_TEXT: Record<string, string> = {
  placed: "Placed",
  paid: "Paid",
  picking: "Being picked",
  shipped: "Shipped",
  delivered: "Delivered",
  ready: "Ready to collect",
  collected: "Collected",
  cancelled: "Cancelled",
};

const TONES: Record<string, "neutral" | "blue" | "amber" | "green" | "sale" | "red"> = {
  placed: "neutral", paid: "blue", picking: "amber", shipped: "blue", delivered: "green",
  ready: "sale", collected: "green", cancelled: "red",
};

export function StatusBadge({ status }: { status: string }) {
  return <Badge tone={TONES[status] ?? "neutral"}>{ORDER_STATUS_TEXT[status] ?? status}</Badge>;
}

/** Every recorded status change, oldest first, with when and who made it (R11). Ordered by seq, never by time:
 *  checkout writes `placed` and `paid` in one transaction, so both rows have the same timestamp. */
export function OrderTimeline({ history }: { history: HistoryEntry[] }) {
  const changes = [...history].sort((a, b) => a.seq - b.seq);
  return (
    <ol className="divide-y divide-neutral-100">
      {changes.map((change) => (
        <li key={change.seq} className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-3 gap-y-1 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm">
          <span className="w-30 shrink-0 text-xs tabular-nums text-neutral-500">
            {formatDateTime(change.changed_at)}
          </span>
          <StatusBadge status={change.to_status} />
          <span className="text-neutral-600">
            {change.from_status ? `from ${ORDER_STATUS_TEXT[change.from_status] ?? change.from_status}` : "order created"}
            {/* No staff member recorded: the customer made the change (at checkout, in this sub-project). */}
            <span className="text-neutral-500"> · by {change.changed_by_name ?? "the customer"}</span>
          </span>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ol>
  );
}

const either = new Intl.ListFormat("en-AU", { type: "disjunction" });

/** The moves the transition table allows from the current status (BR09), as text only: nothing on the storefront
 *  changes an order's status. */
export function NextStatuses({ statuses }: { statuses: string[] }) {
  const names = statuses.map((status) => (ORDER_STATUS_TEXT[status] ?? status).toLowerCase());
  return (
    <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600">
      {names.length === 0 ? "Nothing more happens to this order." : `What happens next: ${either.format(names)}.`}
    </p>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: `web/src/app/(shop)/account/page.tsx`**

Adapted from the branch: `CustomerGate` instead of the demo texts, `reloadCustomer()` after an add or a retire (the
PATCH is idempotent, so retiring twice is answered the same), `toApiError`. Replace the stub with:

```tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";

import { AddressForm } from "@/components/shop/address-form";
import { useShop } from "@/components/shop/context";
import { CustomerGate } from "@/components/shop/customer-gate";
import { Badge, Button, Card, Empty, ErrorBox, Modal } from "@/components/ui";
import { type Address, type ApiError, api, call, type CustomerDetail, toApiError } from "@/lib/api/client";
import { formatDate } from "@/lib/format";

export default function AccountPage() {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <title>My account · JB Hi-Fi</title>
      <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="text-sm text-neutral-500">
        <Link href="/" className="hover:underline">Home</Link> / My account
      </nav>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">My account</h1>
      <CustomerGate what="see your details and addresses">
        {(customer) => <Account customer={customer} />}
      </CustomerGate>
    </div>
  );
}

function Account({ customer }: { customer: CustomerDetail }) {
  const { reloadCustomer } = useShop();
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
  const [retiring, setRetiring] = useState<Address>();
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<string>();
  const [error, setError] = useState<ApiError>();
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);

  async function retire(address: Address) {
    setPending(true);
    setError(undefined);
    try {
      // The only change an address accepts (BR16). Retiring one that is already retired is answered the same.
      await call(api.PATCH("/api/v1/customers/{customer_id}/addresses/{address_no}", {
        params: { path: { customer_id: customer.customer_id, address_no: address.address_no } },
        body: { is_active: false },
      }));
      setRetiring(undefined);
      setNotice(`Address #${address.address_no} (${address.line1}) is retired. Orders already placed keep it.`);
      reloadCustomer();
    } catch (failure) {
      setError(toApiError(failure));
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  }

  const current = customer.addresses.filter((address) => address.is_active);
  const past = customer.addresses.filter((address) => !address.is_active);

  return (
    <>
      {notice && (
        <p role="status" className="rounded-lg border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm
          text-emerald-900">
          {notice}
          <button type="button" className="ml-3 underline" onClick={() => setNotice(undefined)}>dismiss</button>
        </p>
      )}
      {error && !retiring && <ErrorBox error={error} onClose={() => setError(undefined)} />}

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,2fr)]">
        <Card title="Your details">
          <dl className="divide-y divide-neutral-100 text-sm">
            <div className="flex justify-between gap-4 px-4 py-2.5">
              <dt className="text-neutral-500">Name</dt>
              <dd className="font-medium">{customer.full_name}</dd>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between gap-4 px-4 py-2.5">
              <dt className="text-neutral-500">Email</dt>
              <dd className="font-mono text-xs">{customer.email}</dd>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between gap-4 px-4 py-2.5">
              <dt className="text-neutral-500">Phone</dt>
              <dd>{customer.phone ?? "—"}</dd>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between gap-4 px-4 py-2.5">
              <dt className="text-neutral-500">Customer since</dt>
              <dd>{formatDate(customer.created_at)}</dd>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between gap-4 px-4 py-2.5">
              <dt className="text-neutral-500">Customer #</dt>
              <dd className="tabular-nums">{customer.customer_id}</dd>
            </div>
          </dl>
          <p className="border-t border-neutral-200 px-4 py-3 text-xs text-neutral-500">
            The email is unique and stored in lower case (BR01). You sign in with Clerk; the API links that sign-in
            to this record.
          </p>
        </Card>

        <div className="space-y-5">
          <Card title="Current delivery addresses"
            actions={<Button size="sm" onClick={() => setAdding(true)}>Add address</Button>}>
            {current.length === 0
              ? <Empty>No current address. Add one before ordering delivery.</Empty>
              : (
                <ul className="divide-y divide-neutral-100">
                  {current.map((address) => (
                    <li key={address.address_no}
                      className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-3">
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-medium">{address.line1}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-neutral-500">
                          {address.suburb} {address.state} {address.postcode} · #{address.address_no}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <Button size="sm" onClick={() => setRetiring(address)}>Retire…</Button>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              )}
            <p className="border-t border-neutral-200 px-4 py-3 text-xs text-neutral-500">
              Retiring replaces an address without rewriting it: orders already placed keep the address they were
              placed with (BR16), and checkout only offers current ones (BR17).
            </p>
          </Card>

          {past.length > 0 && (
            <Card title="Past addresses">
              <ul className="divide-y divide-neutral-100">
                {past.map((address) => (
                  <li key={address.address_no} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-3 text-sm">
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-neutral-700">{address.line1}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-neutral-500">
                        {address.suburb} {address.state} {address.postcode} · #{address.address_no}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <Badge>retired</Badge>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
              <p className="border-t border-neutral-200 px-4 py-3 text-xs text-neutral-500">
                An address is <b>kept on past orders</b>, which is why it is never edited or deleted.
              </p>
            </Card>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {adding && (
        <AddressForm customer={customer} onCancel={() => setAdding(false)} onAdded={(address) => {
          setAdding(false);
          setNotice(`Address #${address.address_no} added. If it replaces an old one, retire the old one now.`);
          reloadCustomer();
        }} />
      )}

      {retiring && (
        <Modal title="Retire this address?" onClose={() => setRetiring(undefined)} footer={<>
          <Button onClick={() => setRetiring(undefined)}>Keep it</Button>
          <Button tone="danger" disabled={pending} onClick={() => retire(retiring)}>
            {pending ? "Retiring…" : "Retire address"}
          </Button>
        </>}>
          <div className="rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm">
            <div className="font-medium">{retiring.line1}</div>
            <div className="text-neutral-600">{retiring.suburb} {retiring.state} {retiring.postcode}</div>
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm text-neutral-700">
            It stops being offered at checkout straight away. Orders already placed keep it — the record stays on
            file, still pointing at them.
          </p>
          {error && <ErrorBox error={error} />}
        </Modal>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```

Adapted from the branch: `CustomerGate`, rows typed as `OrderSummary`, and only this customer's rows (main's
`useLoad` keeps the previous result while a new key loads). "Ready to collect" stays first; `ready` appears once SP2's
staff moves exist. Replace the stub with:

```tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";

import { CustomerGate } from "@/components/shop/customer-gate";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/shop/order-timeline";
import { Button, Card, Empty, ErrorBox } from "@/components/ui";
import { api, call, type CustomerDetail, type OrderSummary } from "@/lib/api/client";
import { formatDateTime, money } from "@/lib/format";
import { SHOP_PAGES } from "@/lib/shop-pages";
import { useLoad } from "@/lib/use-load";

export default function OrdersPage() {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <title>My orders · JB Hi-Fi</title>
      <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="text-sm text-neutral-500">
        <Link href="/" className="hover:underline">Home</Link> / My orders
      </nav>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">My orders</h1>
        <Link href={SHOP_PAGES.catalogue.href} className="text-sm font-medium hover:underline">
          Shop all products →
        </Link>
      </div>
      <CustomerGate what="see your orders">{(customer) => <Orders customer={customer} />}</CustomerGate>
    </div>
  );
}

function Orders({ customer }: { customer: CustomerDetail }) {
  const customerId = customer.customer_id;
  const orders = useLoad(`orders:${customerId}`, () =>
    call(api.GET("/api/v1/customers/{customer_id}/orders", { params: { path: { customer_id: customerId } } })));

  if (orders.error) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <ErrorBox error={orders.error} />
        <Button onClick={orders.reload}>Try again</Button>
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (!orders.data) return <div className="h-32 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-neutral-200" />;

  // Newest first, as the API lists them (v_order_summary). useLoad keeps the last result while the next one
  // loads, so keep to this customer's orders.
  const rows = orders.data.filter((order) => order.customer_id === customerId);
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return (
      <Card><Empty>
        No orders yet. <Link href={SHOP_PAGES.catalogue.href} className="font-medium underline">Start shopping →</Link>
      </Empty></Card>
    );
  }
  const ready = rows.filter((order) => order.status === "ready");
  const rest = rows.filter((order) => order.status !== "ready");

  return (
    <>
      {ready.length > 0 && (
        <section>
          <h2 className="mb-2 text-lg font-semibold tracking-tight">Ready to collect</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
            {ready.map((order) => <OrderCard key={order.order_id} order={order} highlight />)}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}
      {rest.length > 0 && (
        <section>
          {ready.length > 0 && <h2 className="mb-2 text-lg font-semibold tracking-tight">Earlier orders</h2>}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
            {rest.map((order) => <OrderCard key={order.order_id} order={order} />)}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

function OrderCard({ order, highlight = false }: { order: OrderSummary; highlight?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <Link href={`${SHOP_PAGES.orders.href}/${order.order_id}`}
      className={`block rounded-xl bg-white p-4 shadow-sm ring-1 transition hover:shadow-md ${
        highlight ? "ring-2 ring-yellow-400" : "ring-neutral-200"}`}>
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
        <div>
          <div className="font-semibold">Order #{order.order_id}</div>
          <div className="text-xs text-neutral-500">
            {formatDateTime(order.placed_at)}
            {order.last_updated_at !== order.placed_at && <> · updated {formatDateTime(order.last_updated_at)}</>}
          </div>
        </div>
        <StatusBadge status={order.status} />
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 text-sm text-neutral-600">
        {order.line_count} {order.line_count === 1 ? "item" : "items"} ·{" "}
        {order.fulfilment_method === "delivery" ? "Delivery" : "Click & Collect"} · {order.fulfilling_store_name}
      </div>
      <div className="mt-2 text-lg font-bold tabular-nums">{money(order.total)}</div>
      {highlight && (
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs font-medium text-amber-700">
          <b>{order.fulfilling_store_name}</b> has it ready for you.
        </p>
      )}
    </Link>
  );
}
```

```tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useParams } from "next/navigation";

import { SignedInGate } from "@/components/shop/customer-gate";
import { NextStatuses, OrderTimeline, StatusBadge } from "@/components/shop/order-timeline";
import { Button, Card, Empty, ErrorBox } from "@/components/ui";
import { api, call, type Me } from "@/lib/api/client";
import { formatDateTime, money } from "@/lib/format";
import { SHOP_PAGES } from "@/lib/shop-pages";
import { useLoad } from "@/lib/use-load";

export default function OrderPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const orderId = Number(id);
  const valid = Number.isInteger(orderId) && orderId > 0;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <title>{valid ? `Order #${orderId} · JB Hi-Fi` : "Order · JB Hi-Fi"}</title>
      <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="text-sm text-neutral-500">
        <Link href="/" className="hover:underline">Home</Link> /{" "}
        <Link href={SHOP_PAGES.orders.href} className="hover:underline">My orders</Link> / {valid ? `#${orderId}` : "…"}
      </nav>
      {valid
        ? <SignedInGate what="see this order">{(me) => <Order orderId={orderId} me={me} />}</SignedInGate>
        : (
          <Card><Empty>
            That isn&apos;t an order number.{" "}
            <Link href={SHOP_PAGES.orders.href} className="underline">Back to My orders</Link>.
          </Empty></Card>
        )}
    </div>
  );
}

function Order({ orderId, me }: { orderId: number; me: Me }) {
  // Whoever is signed in asks, and the API decides: customers see their own orders, store staff their store's,
  // support every order. A refusal comes before "not found", so it doesn't reveal which orders exist.
  const order = useLoad(`order:${orderId}:${me.kind}:${me.id}`, () =>
    call(api.GET("/api/v1/orders/{order_id}", { params: { path: { order_id: orderId } } })));
  // useLoad keeps the last result while the next one loads: never show another order under this number.
  const detail = order.data?.order_id === orderId ? order.data : undefined;

  if (order.error) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <ErrorBox error={order.error} />
        <Button onClick={order.reload}>Try again</Button>
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (!detail) return <div className="h-40 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-neutral-200" />;

  return (
    <>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Order #{detail.order_id}</h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-neutral-600">
            Placed {formatDateTime(detail.placed_at)} ·{" "}
            {detail.fulfilment_method === "delivery"
              ? <>Delivery from {detail.fulfilling_store_name}</>
              : <>Click &amp; Collect from {detail.fulfilling_store_name}</>}
          </p>
          <NextStatuses statuses={detail.allowed_next_statuses} />
        </div>
        <StatusBadge status={detail.status} />
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,2fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
        <Card title="Items" actions={<span className="text-xs text-neutral-500">prices as sold (BR08)</span>}>
          <ul className="divide-y divide-neutral-100">
            {detail.lines.map((line) => (
              <li key={line.line_no} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-3">
                <div>
                  <Link href={`${SHOP_PAGES.catalogue.href}/${line.product_id}`} className="font-medium hover:underline">
                    {line.name}
                  </Link>
                  <div className="font-mono text-xs text-neutral-500">{line.sku}</div>
                </div>
                <div className="text-right text-sm">
                  <div className="tabular-nums">{money(line.unit_price)} × {line.quantity}</div>
                  <div className="font-semibold tabular-nums">{money(line.line_total)}</div>
                </div>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-neutral-200 px-4 py-3">
            <span className="text-sm text-neutral-600">Order total</span>
            <span className="text-lg font-bold tabular-nums">{money(detail.total)}</span>
          </div>
        </Card>

        <div className="space-y-5">
          <Card title="Timeline" actions={<span className="text-xs text-neutral-500">R11</span>}>
            <OrderTimeline history={detail.history} />
          </Card>

          <Card title="Where it is going">
            <div className="px-4 py-3 text-sm">
              {detail.delivery_address
                ? (
                  <>
                    <div className="font-medium">{detail.delivery_address.line1}</div>
                    <div className="text-neutral-600">
                      {detail.delivery_address.suburb} {detail.delivery_address.state}{" "}
                      {detail.delivery_address.postcode}
                    </div>
                    <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-neutral-500">
                      Address #{detail.delivery_address.address_no}, as it was when the order was placed: addresses
                      are never edited (BR16).
                    </p>
                  </>
                )
                : <p className="text-neutral-600">Collect from <b>{detail.fulfilling_store_name}</b>. Bring your order number.</p>}
            </div>
          </Card>

          <Card title="Payment (simulated)" actions={<span className="text-xs text-neutral-500">BR14, BR20</span>}>
            <dl className="divide-y divide-neutral-100 text-sm">
              <Amount label="Charged" value={detail.payment.charged} />
              <Amount label="Refunded" value={detail.payment.refunded} />
              <Amount label="Balance (charged − refunded)" value={detail.payment.balance} strong />
            </dl>
          </Card>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

/** Money from the API (a decimal string, from the v_order_payment view): formatted, never added up here. */
function Amount({ label, value, strong = false }: { label: string; value: string; strong?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 px-4 py-2.5">
      <dt className="text-neutral-600">{label}</dt>
      <dd className={`tabular-nums ${strong ? "font-semibold" : ""}`}>{money(value)}</dd>
    </div>
  );
}
```
